Bathinda-151 001, the 19th January 1987 

No. ZO/BTI/eS/S,—Notice in respect of Officers of our 
Bank who were transferred to this Zone and joined their 
respective Places of; Postings as mentioned against each 
during the period from 1-9-1986 to 31-12-1986. 

1. Shri Ourbir Singh Sandhu, Officer JMGS-I joined 
Zonal Office, Bathlnda on 10-9-1986 as Asstt. 
Security Officer on his new appointment. 

2. Shri Yashwaht Singh Officer JMGS-I Asstt. Account¬ 
ant of Fazilka branch was transferred and posted at 
Alamwala branch as Manager and ho joined thereat 
on 10-9-1986.

Kazheri branch was transferred and posted at 
Bathinda (GNTP) branch as such and he Joined 
thereat on 15-9-1986.

from Zonal Office, Bathinda and posted at Bathinda 
(MSC) branch as Asstt. AcetL and he joined thereat 
on 15-9-1986.

Alamwala branch was transferred and posted at 
Jaitu branch as Manager and he Joined thereat on 
22-9-1986.

branch was transferred and posted at Moga branch 
as Accountant and he joined thereat on 22-9-1986.

(Bathinda) was transferred and posted at Regional 
Qffice-I (Bathinda) and he joined thereat on 8-19-1986.

Kalyan Sukha branch was transferred and posted 
at Vir Kalan branch as such and he joined thereat 
on 11-10-1986.

Bathinda (IE) branch was transferred and posted at

15-10-1986.

[Pakt III— -Sec. 4 

OFFICE OF THE CENTRAL PROVIDENT FUND 
COMMISSIONER 


INDUSTRIAL DEVELOPMENT BANK OF INDIA 
Bombay, the 13tb January 1987 

No. 1ICD(51)/445/IFC-6.—In pursuance of Rule 4 of 
the Industrial Finance Corporation Rules, 1965, the Indus¬ 
trial Development Bank of India (Development Bank) 
hereby notifies that the Industrial Finance Corporation of 
India (Corporation) has, with the prior approval of the 
Development Bank, introduced a Scheme under which tm 
Industrial unit with export sales of 25% or above in relation 
to its total sates of own manufactured goods will be entitled 
to an interest rebate to the extent of l/5th (or 20%) of 
its interest payment on the rupee loans provided by the 
Corporation. The rebate will be subject to floor interest 
rate of 10% on relative loans. The hundred per cent 
Export Oriented Units which have been sanaioned assistance 
under the earlier 1,5% interest rebate scheme will have an 
optioft to condmie to clakn the rebate uader the earlier 
scheaie or choose the benefit of the new scheme. The new 
scheme w31 be applicable to all loans giantcd on or after 
the 1st December, 1986. 

S. M. PALIA, Executive Director. 


THE INSTITUTE OF CAST AND WORKS ACCOUNT- 
ANTS Oe INDIA 

Calcutta, the 22ndi December 1986 

No. 16'CWR(732)/86,—la pursuance of Regulation 16 
of the Cost and Works Accounlants Regulations, 1959,

New Delhi, the 16th January 1987 

No. P.lV/U3)/86.—hi exercise of the powers ctmferred 
by sub-section (7) of section 5D of the Employees’ Provi¬ 
dent Funds and Miscellaneous Provisions Act, 1952 (19 
1952), the Central Board with the approval of the Central 
Government, hereby makes the following regulations for 
regulating the method of recruitment to the post of Binder 
under the Employees’ Provident Fund Organisation, 
namely :— 

1. Short title and commencement. These regulations 
may be called the Employees’ Provident Fund Organisation 
(Binder) Recruitment Regulations, 1986. 

(2) These regulations shall come into force on the date of 
their publication in the Official Gazette. 

2. Number, clasilfication and icalc of pay. —The number 
of posts, their classification and the scale of pay attached 
thcreto'shall be as specified in columns (2) to (4) of the 
Schedule annexed to these regulations. 

3. Method of recruitment, age limit and other qualifi- 
ficallons etc. —The method of recruitment, age limit, quali¬ 
fications and other matters connected therewith shall be as 
specified in columns (5) to (14) of the Schedule aforesaid. 

4. Disqualifications. —No, person ;— 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage with 
a person having a spouse living, or 

(b) who, having a spouse living, has entered into or 
contracted a marriage with any person, 

shall be eligible for appointment,to the said post : 

Provided that the Central Government or the Central 
Provident Fund Commissioner may, if satisfied that such 
marriage is permissible under the personal law applicable 
to such person and the other party to the marriage and 
that there are other grounds for so doing, exempt any person 
from the operation of this rule. 

5. Power to lelas.. —Where the Central Provident Fund 
Commissioner is of the opinion that it is necessary or ex¬ 
pedient so to do, he may, with the approval of the Central 
Government, by order, for reasons to be recorded in writing, 
relax any of the provisions of these regulations with respect 
to any class of category of person. 



Part III— Sbc. 4] THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, JANUARY 31, 1987 (MAGHA ll, 1908) 775 

6. Savinas,—Nothing in those regulations shall alfcct reservations, relaxation of age limit and other nconcessionj 
required to bo provided for the Scheduled Castes, the Scheduled Tribes, the Ex-sorvicemen and other special categories Of 
persons in accordance with the orders issued by the Central Government, from time to time, in this regard.

PUNE CANTONMENT BOARD 
Pune, the 30th December 1986 

No. S.R.O.1/2/2.5,—^Whereas the draft of amendment for 
imposing entertainment tax was published in the Conton- 
ment Board’s) Notice No. 1/2/25, dated the 13th May, 1985 
as required by Section 61 of the Cantonments Aqt, 1924 
(2 of 1924) for inviting objections and suggestions from 
all the persons likely to be affected thereby within a period 
of 30 days from the date of publication of the said notice; 

And whereas the said notice was put on the Cantonment 
Notice Board on the 7tb May, 1985; 

And whereas objections and suggestions received from 
the public by Cantonment Board within the aforesaid period 
have been duly considered by the Cantomjient Board and 
it was resolved that the rates proposed are reasonable; 

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by 
Seaion 60 of the Cantonments Act, 1924, the Poona 
Cantonment Board with the previous sanction of the Central 
Government, hereby makes the following amendment, 
namely :— 


AMENDMENT 

In the Notification of the Government of Bombay, General 
Department No. 4160, dated the 17th June, 1918, tmder 
the heading V. TAX ON ENTERTAINMENTS 

for item No.. 1, the following item shall be substituted, 
namely :— 

"(11 Cinema Talkies having seating capacity more than 
five hundred seats :— 

(i) in civil area Rs. 30/- per show. 

(ii) outside civil area Rs. 10/- per show." 

NOTE :—The original notification of the Government 
of Bombay dated 17-6-1918 was published in Bombay 
Government Gazette 1918 PT. I at page No. 1143 and was 
subsequently amended by the follO'Wmg notifications, 
namely ;— 

1. S,R.O. No. 228, dt. 10-5-51 published in Gazette of 
India, dated 19-5-1951. 

2. S.R.O. No. 268, dated 23-6-1951. 

3. S.R.O. No. 46. dated 1-2-1969. 

Cantonment Executive Officer, Poona. 

R. K. BRAR
